[codex] ## Operating Rules
[codex] 
[codex] - Do not use agent or subagent tools for code exploration. Inspect the workspace
[codex]   directly with `rg`, `find`, and file reads.
[codex] - Never run Git write commands, including `commit`, `push`, `checkout`, `reset`,
[codex]   or branch manipulation, unless the user explicitly requests them. The user
[codex]   controls Git operations.
[codex] - Keep implementations simple. Choose the most direct design that handles
[codex]   realistic inputs, states, and failure modes without weakening security, data
[codex]   integrity, or required behavior.
[codex] - Put request shape and syntax validation at the API boundary. Put business
[codex]   invariants, ownership, persistence state, and cross-entity rules in services.
[codex] - Do not duplicate validation across layers unless another caller can bypass the
[codex]   validated boundary or the service owns the rule.
[codex] - Add defensive branches only for states that can plausibly arise and have a
[codex]   useful response. Handle failures at external and asynchronous boundaries
[codex]   explicitly.
[codex] - Never commit credentials or replace missing infrastructure, credentials, or
[codex]   shared artifacts with hard-coded workarounds.

[codex] ## Architecture and Security Rules
[codex] 
[codex] - Keep controllers thin and delegate application behavior to services.
[codex]   Controllers must never import repositories.
[codex] - Keep consumers thin and delegate to services. Consumers must never import
[codex]   repositories.
[codex] - Keep transaction boundaries in the service layer and persistence behind
[codex]   repositories.
[codex] - Use Jakarta Persistence APIs only. Never import `org.hibernate.*` or depend on
[codex]   Hibernate-specific behavior.
[codex] - Extend the shared auditable or soft-deletable base entity that matches the
[codex]   domain lifecycle; consult the shared Spring Boot conventions before creating
[codex]   an entity.
[codex] - Access external exchange-rate systems through `ExchangeRateProvider`. The
[codex]   service layer must never reference FRED-specific clients or types.
[codex] - Publish domain events from services. Do not inject message publishers into
[codex]   services; bridge domain events to external messages in listeners.
[codex] - Apply `@SchedulerLock` to scheduled work that must execute once across pods.
[codex] - Cache read paths only where the documented cache strategy calls for it, and
[codex]   evict affected cache entries after imports or mutations.
[codex] - Preserve claims-header-based authorization and enforce endpoint permissions
[codex]   with `@PreAuthorize`. Do not trust client-supplied identity or permission
[codex]   headers outside the platform's validated gateway path.
[codex] - Treat role definitions and atomic permission ownership as permission-service
[codex]   concerns. Do not redefine the ecosystem RBAC model in this repository.
[codex]

[codex]

[codex] [code-quality-standards.md](../service-common/docs/code-quality-standards.md).
[codex] Do not skip this step. In particular, use `var` where the standard requires it,
[codex] use descriptive names, avoid wildcard imports, and end the first sentence of
[codex] Javadoc with a period.

[codex] Before writing or changing tests, read the relevant section of
[codex] [testing-patterns.md](../service-common/docs/testing-patterns.md):

[codex]   cache, or application-context tests.

[codex]   them.

[codex] - Keep reusable test values in the repository's test fixtures or constants

[codex] 
[codex] ### Workspace
[codex] 
[codex] ../currency-service
[codex] 
[codex] ### Goal
[codex] 
[codex] Provide dependency update proposals and resolved vulnerability alerts for this service.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Scope
[codex] 
[codex] Repository Renovate config, dependency-submission workflow and nearest docs.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Non-goals
[codex] 
[codex] No dependency upgrades, service logic changes, releases or sibling writes.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Required context
[codex] 
[codex] Read local `AGENTS.md`, build/catalog/workflows, the shared preset, and
[codex] `../orchestration/docs/development/service-common-artifact-resolution.md`.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Execution steps
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Add the thin shared-preset config and verify Gradle, wrapper, image and Actions
[codex]    discovery, including the declared `serviceCommon` dependency.
[codex] 2. Apply the official dependency-submission pattern from Phase 3 with basic caching
[codex]    and references to this repo's existing GitHub Packages secret names. Keep
[codex]    package-read credentials distinct from the submission token in the workflow;
[codex]    do not verify or obtain either credential during this phase.
[codex] 3. Configure application/runtime/test graph coverage and document the trusted
[codex]    branch schedule, bot PR validation behavior, and Phase 12 evidence handoff.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Implementation notes
[codex] 
[codex] A historical remote-package build may be recorded if already available, but is
[codex] neither required nor proof that the new submission workflow works. Full remote
[codex] resolution belongs to Phase 12. Do not substitute cached/local artifacts for that
[codex] proof or omit the dependency.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Validation
[codex] 
[codex] Run the config validator, extraction, credential-free lookup and actionlint.
[codex] Inspect a local snapshot only where generation works without authentication;
[codex] record missing runtime/test coverage and authentication-dependent build checks as
[codex] pending Phase 12. A complete snapshot and hosted submission are not local gates.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Completion criteria
[codex] 
[codex] Repo-local configuration and credential-free checks pass, declared internal
[codex] coordinates are extracted, and each authenticated check has a durable Phase 12
[codex] handoff. The phase completes with remote resolution, complete runtime/test graph
[codex] coverage, and hosted submission explicitly pending; credentials are not required.

[codex] # Service-Common Artifact Resolution
[codex] 
[codex] This document explains the split between local `mavenLocal()` resolution and
[codex] the GitHub Packages path used by GitHub Actions and release builds.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Version Scope
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` is a shared Java library version, not the deployment version
[codex] for the whole Budget Analyzer stack. Bump and release it when
[codex] `spring-platform`, `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, or `service-web`
[codex] changes. Do not change it only because a service image, frontend artifact,
[codex] route, production overlay, `/api-docs` metadata file, or OCI deployment
[codex] configuration changed.
[codex] 
[codex] Java services can release runtime artifacts while continuing to consume the
[codex] same checked-in `serviceCommon` coordinate from `gradle/libs.versions.toml`.
[codex] The production deployment identity is the digest-pinned image inventory and
[codex] deployment manifest, which may contain mixed service artifact versions.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Local Contributor Flow
[codex] 
[codex] The supported side-by-side workspace flow must stay credential-free.
[codex] 
[codex] If you follow [getting-started.md](getting-started.md) and run local
[codex] development through orchestration and Tilt:
[codex] 
[codex] - clone the repos side by side
[codex] - open the sibling `orchestration/` repo
[codex] - run `./setup.sh`
[codex] - run `tilt up`
[codex] 
[codex] you should not need `GITHUB_ACTOR`, `GITHUB_TOKEN`, or a personal access token
[codex] just to start the app locally.
[codex] 
[codex] That works because the local build path stays local-first:
[codex] 
[codex] - Java service repos keep `mavenLocal()` first
[codex] - Tilt runs the `service-common-publish` resource first
[codex] - `service-common-publish` runs `publishToMavenLocal`
[codex] - downstream host-side Gradle builds then resolve `service-common` from Maven
[codex]   Local
[codex] 
[codex] If you hit a local `service-common` resolution error while working in the full
[codex] workspace, republish it locally and rerun the service build:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] cd ../service-common
[codex] ./gradlew clean build publishToMavenLocal
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ## When GitHub Packages Credentials Are Required
[codex] 
[codex] GitHub Packages credentials are a CI/release concern, not a getting-started
[codex] prerequisite.
[codex] 
[codex] You need GitHub Packages credentials when all of these are true:
[codex] 
[codex] - the build is not using the local orchestration/Tilt path
[codex] - `service-common` is not already available through Maven Local
[codex] - the build must resolve the published `org.budgetanalyzer` artifacts remotely
[codex]   from `https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common`
[codex] 
[codex] Typical examples:
[codex] 
[codex] - default GitHub Actions `build.yml` runs in `transaction-service`,
[codex]   `currency-service`, `permission-service`, and `session-gateway`
[codex] - release-image Docker builds
[codex] - isolated CI or clean-builder image builds
[codex] - manual clean-shell verification of remote published artifacts
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` itself now refreshes the current checked-in snapshot from
[codex] `main` through its `publish-snapshot.yml` workflow. That remote snapshot path
[codex] exists to support isolated CI/release builds; it does not change the local
[codex] contributor contract.
[codex] 
[codex] For the Java consumer repos, the default build workflow contract is now:
[codex] 
[codex] - read the pinned `serviceCommon` version from `gradle/libs.versions.toml`
[codex] - fail fast if `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` or
[codex]   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` is missing
[codex] - preflight the versioned `service-core` and `service-web` POM URLs in GitHub
[codex]   Packages
[codex] - run `./gradlew build` with `GITHUB_ACTOR` / `GITHUB_TOKEN` exported from
[codex]   that same secret pair
[codex] 
[codex] ## GitHub Actions Environment
[codex] 
[codex] When a workflow intentionally resolves `service-common` remotely from GitHub
[codex] Packages, Gradle needs both a token and a package username.
[codex] 
[codex] For cross-repo private Maven/Gradle consumption, do not assume the workflow
[codex] repo's own `GITHUB_TOKEN` can read `service-common`. GitHub still treats these
[codex] packages as repository-scoped, so the consuming workflow needs an explicit
[codex] GitHub Packages credential.
[codex] 
[codex] In GitHub Actions, the current repo wiring expects:
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex] env:
[codex]   GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex]   GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] `GITHUB_ACTOR` here means the username that owns the GitHub Packages credential,
[codex] not necessarily `${{ github.actor }}`. `GITHUB_TOKEN` here means the secret used
[codex] for remote package reads, not necessarily the workflow repo's default token.
[codex] This requirement applies to the remote GitHub Packages path used by default
[codex] `build.yml` and release workflows. It does not apply to the normal `tilt up`
[codex] contributor flow.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Containerized Release Builds
[codex] 
[codex] The Java service Dockerfiles now support remote `service-common` resolution for
[codex] release and isolated CI builds without copying host `.m2` state into the build
[codex] context and without checking out sibling source trees.
[codex] 
[codex] Pass the workflow credentials into BuildKit as secrets:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] docker build \
[codex]   --secret id=github_actor,env=GITHUB_ACTOR \
[codex]   --secret id=github_token,env=GITHUB_TOKEN \
[codex]   -f Dockerfile .
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] In GitHub Actions, the release job should still expose those same credential
[codex] values under the env names the current Gradle builds read:
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex] env:
[codex]   GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex]   GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] The Dockerfiles read those values only inside the Gradle builder-stage `RUN`
[codex] steps, so the credentials are not written into the image, filesystem layers, or
[codex] checked-in repo files.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Why Public Package Visibility Does Not Remove This
[codex] 
[codex] Making the `service-common` Maven packages public in the GitHub UI does not
[codex] remove the Maven/Gradle authentication requirement.
[codex] 
[codex] Keep the release workflow package-visibility and workflow-access cleanup, but do not
[codex] treat it as the fix for local contributor UX. The fix for local contributor UX
[codex] is keeping the side-by-side workspace and Tilt flow local-first and
[codex] credential-free.
[codex] 
[codex] exec
[codex] /bin/bash -lc 'find . -name AGENTS.md -print' in /workspace/currency-service
[codex]  succeeded in 0ms:
[codex] ./AGENTS.md
[codex] 
[codex] exec
[codex] /bin/bash -lc "node --version && npm --version && sed -n '1,260p' build.gradle.kts && sed -n '1,260p' gradle/libs.versions.toml && for f in .github/workflows/*; do printf '\\nFILE %s\\n' \""'$f"; sed -n '"'1,260p' \""'$f"; done' in /workspace/currency-service
[codex]  succeeded in 0ms:
[codex] v24.20.0

[codex]         id: release

[codex]           SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}

[codex]               exit 1
[codex]             fi

[codex]             exit 1
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           docker_label="${requested_docker_label:-${default_docker_label}}"

[codex]             exit 1

[codex]           if [[ -z "${SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex]             echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex]             exit 1
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           echo "docker_label=${docker_label}" >> "${GITHUB_OUTPUT}"

[codex]       - name: Resolve checked-out revision
[codex]         id: source
[codex]         shell: bash
[codex]         run: |
[codex]           set -euo pipefail
[codex] 
[codex]           commit_sha="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"

[codex] # Dependency Automation
[codex] 
[codex] The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] failure triage are owned by
[codex] [orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Update discovery
[codex] 
[codex] `renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] `serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] `service-web`.
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] 
[codex] The Phase 4 local extraction found 57 dependency occurrences across eight files:
[codex] 37 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence, three Dockerfile
[codex] occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three workflows. Public
[codex] Gradle, wrapper, and image lookups produced candidate updates. GitHub Actions
[codex] lookups and the three `org.budgetanalyzer` Maven coordinates remain
[codex] authenticated hosted checks; the local runner had no GitHub or package-read
[codex] token, so those checks are pending rather than passed.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] `serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] fixed.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] 
[codex] `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] `basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] 
[codex] Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] `GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] `${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] 
[codex] The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] `service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Bot pull request checks
[codex] 
[codex] Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] approval.
[codex] 
[codex] Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] ./gradlew clean build
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] `pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] # Currency Service
[codex] 
[codex] > "Archetype: service. Role: Manages currencies and exchange rates with external provider integration."
[codex] >
[codex] > — [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md#tree-position)
[codex] 
[codex] [![Build](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/currency-service/actions/workflows/build.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/currency-service/actions/workflows/build.yml)
[codex] 
[codex] Spring Boot microservice that automates exchange rate imports from the [Federal Reserve Economic Data (FRED)](https://fred.stlouisfed.org/) API, delivering reliable currency and exchange rate data for Budget Analyzer.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Core Capabilities
[codex] 
[codex] - **Automated FRED imports** - Daily scheduled imports with provider abstraction ([details](docs/fred-integration.md))
[codex] - **Currency series management** - CRUD operations for exchange rate time series ([API docs](docs/api/README.md))
[codex] - **Historical rate queries** - Date range filtering with Redis caching (80-95% hit rate)
[codex] - **Distributed coordination** - Multi-pod safe scheduling with ShedLock
[codex] - **Event-driven architecture** - Guaranteed delivery via transactional outbox + RabbitMQ
[codex] 
[codex] ## Quick Start
[codex] 
[codex] **Prerequisites:** JDK 25, Docker/Docker Compose
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] # 1. Start infrastructure
[codex] cd ../orchestration && tilt up
[codex] 
[codex] # 2. Export credentials and start the service
[codex] cd ../currency-service
[codex] export FRED_API_KEY=your_api_key_here
[codex] export SPRING_DATASOURCE_PASSWORD=your_db_password
[codex] export SPRING_RABBITMQ_PASSWORD=your_rabbitmq_password
[codex] export SPRING_DATA_REDIS_PASSWORD=your_redis_password
[codex] # See docs/local-development.md for full env var list (TLS, ports, etc.)
[codex] ./gradlew bootRun
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] **Dev server:** `http://localhost:8084` | **Swagger UI:** `http://localhost:8084/swagger-ui.html`
[codex] 
[codex] ## Technology Stack
[codex] 
[codex] Java 25, Spring Boot 3.x (Web, Data JPA, Modulith, Actuator), PostgreSQL, Redis, RabbitMQ, ShedLock, Flyway, SpringDoc OpenAPI, TestContainers + JUnit 5
[codex] 
[codex] ## Build and Test
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./gradlew clean build
[codex] ./gradlew test jacocoTestReport
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] JaCoCo coverage reports are written to
[codex] `build/reports/jacoco/test/html/index.html` and
[codex] `build/reports/jacoco/test/jacocoTestReport.xml`. `check` enforces the
[codex] configured coverage gates.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Project Structure
[codex] 
[codex] ```
[codex] currency-service/
[codex] ├── src/main/java/              # Java source code
[codex] ├── src/main/resources/
[codex] │   ├── application.yml
[codex] │   └── db/migration/           # Flyway migrations
[codex] ├── src/test/java/              # Unit and integration tests
[codex] └── docs/                       # Detailed documentation
[codex] ```
[codex]

[codex] RUN --mount=type=cache,target=/root/.gradle \
[codex]     --mount=type=secret,id=github_actor,required=false \

[codex] 1856:[codex] [codex] # The `dependency-submission` action
[codex] 1858:[codex] [codex] The `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action provides the simplest (and recommended) way to generate a 
[codex] 1862:[codex] [codex] The dependency graph snapshot is generated via integration with the [GitHub Dependency Graph Gradle Plugin](https://plugins.gradle.org/plugin/org.gradle.github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin), and submitted to your repository via the 
[codex] 1863:[codex] [codex] [GitHub Dependency Submission API](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/dependency-graph/dependency-submission).
[codex] 1864:[codex] [codex] The generated snapshot files can be submitted in the same job, or saved for submission in a subsequent job.

[codex] # Dependency Automation
[codex] 
[codex] The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] failure triage are owned by
[codex] [orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] This document records only the `service-common` integration and review checks.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Update discovery
[codex] 
[codex] `renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, and GitHub Actions managers discover this repository's
[codex] version catalog, build scripts, wrapper distribution, and workflow actions.
[codex] There are currently no Dockerfiles in this repository. Add file-specific
[codex] manager configuration only if a future dependency declaration is not covered
[codex] by a native manager.
[codex] 
[codex] The Phase 3 local extraction found 52 dependency occurrences across 12 files:
[codex] 33 Gradle occurrences in seven files, one wrapper occurrence, and 18 GitHub
[codex] Actions occurrences in four workflows. Maven and Gradle lookups produced 41
[codex] candidate branches before scheduling, concurrency limits, and dashboard
[codex] approval. Maintained-line and later-major proposals were both visible for the
[codex] Spring platform, documentation, test, locking, and wrapper dependencies. The
[codex] Actions references were extracted, but authenticated GitHub lookups remain a
[codex] post-publication hosted check because no GitHub token is supplied to local
[codex] validation.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. It does not turn every
[codex] version inherited from the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, or Spring Modulith BOMs
[codex] into an independently updateable declaration. The resolved dependency graph
[codex] described below is the security inventory for those inherited versions.
[codex] 
[codex] Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] In particular, Spring Boot 4 and the related Spring Cloud, Spring Framework,
[codex] Spring Security, Spring Modulith, SpringDoc, Testcontainers, ShedLock, and test
[codex] platform lines are a compatibility migration, not a routine patch. Renovate
[codex] cannot select the correct release-train combination or coordinate consumer
[codex] services by itself.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] 
[codex] `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] `basic` cache provider. Its job token receives only `contents: write`, which is
[codex] required by GitHub's Dependency Submission API. The graph is submitted
[codex] directly and is not retained as a workflow artifact or published as a Build
[codex] Scan.
[codex] 
[codex] The action's default resolution task visits all projects and all resolvable
[codex] configurations. Do not add project or configuration filters without proving
[codex] equivalent coverage: the platform modules contribute imported BOMs, while the
[codex] library modules contribute compile, runtime, and test dependency trees.
[codex] 
[codex] Local Phase 3 validation generated an official plugin snapshot without
[codex] submitting it. The snapshot contained 216 resolved coordinate/version entries
[codex] across the four subprojects. It included the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and
[codex] Spring Modulith BOM coordinates and these inherited component families where
[codex] the current build actually resolves them:
[codex] 
[codex] | Component family | Resolution evidence |
[codex] | --- | --- |
[codex] | Spring Framework | Library runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] | Spring Security | `service-core` and `service-web` test classpaths; compile-only APIs are also resolved through compile classpaths |
[codex] | Embedded Tomcat | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the servlet stack |
[codex] | Netty | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the reactive stack |
[codex] | Jackson | `service-core` and `service-web` runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] 
[codex] The entry count and resolved versions will change as the checked-in dependency
[codex] selection changes; they are evidence from onboarding, not a desired-version
[codex] inventory. A successful local snapshot proves generation and transitive
[codex] coverage, but only a hosted run after repository activation proves submission
[codex] and creates Dependabot alert input.
[codex] 
[codex] GitHub's dependency graph reports resolved packages but does not decide which
[codex] BOM or override should remediate an inherited finding. A Dependabot alert is
[codex] also not proof that an available Spring Boot update fixes every Framework,
[codex] Security, Jackson, Tomcat, or Netty advisory. Trace the affected configuration
[codex] with Gradle dependency insight and review any override as a service-owner
[codex] decision.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Bot pull request checks
[codex] 
[codex] For every Renovate pull request:
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Keep the shared preset's no-automerge and dashboard-approval behavior.
[codex] 2. Review the resolved dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring
[codex]    release-train compatibility, and whether an inherited advisory is actually
[codex]    remediated.
[codex] 3. Run the repository-required validation in order:
[codex] 
[codex]    ```bash
[codex]    ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex]    ./gradlew clean build
[codex]    ```
[codex] 
[codex] 4. For platform or shared-library changes, validate all consuming services
[codex]    before release as required by
[codex]    [versioning and compatibility](versioning-and-compatibility.md). Do not
[codex]    publish artifacts or update consumers from an unreviewed bot branch.
[codex] 
[codex] Graph generation failures are failures, not clean security results. Preserve
[codex] dependency-resolution and submission errors for triage; do not substitute
[codex] Maven Local, omit configurations, or enable a hosted Build Scan to make the job
[codex] pass.
[codex]

[codex] [codex] @@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
[codex] [codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] [codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] [codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] [codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] [codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] [codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] [codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] [codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] [codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] [codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] [codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] [codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] [codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] [codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] [codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] [codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] [codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] [codex] +fixed.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] [codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] [codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] [codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] [codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] [codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] [codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] [codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] [codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] [codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] [codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] [codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] [codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] [codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] [codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] [codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] [codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] [codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] [codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] [codex] +approval.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +```bash
[codex] [codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] [codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] [codex] +```
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] [codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] [codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] [codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] [codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] [codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] [codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] [codex] new file mode 100644

[codex] [codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] [codex] +++ b/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] [codex] @@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
[codex] [codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +on:
[codex] [codex] +  push:
[codex] [codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] [codex] +  schedule:
[codex] [codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] [codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +env:
[codex] [codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +jobs:
[codex] [codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] [codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] [codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] [codex] +    permissions:
[codex] [codex] +      contents: write
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +    steps:
[codex] [codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] [codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6

[codex] [codex] @@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
[codex] [codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] [codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] [codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] [codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] [codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] [codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] [codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] [codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] [codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] [codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] [codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] [codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] [codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] [codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] [codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] [codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] [codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] [codex] +fixed.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] [codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] [codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] [codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] [codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] [codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] [codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] [codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] [codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] [codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] [codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] [codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] [codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] [codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] [codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] [codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] [codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] [codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] [codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] [codex] +approval.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +```bash
[codex] [codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] [codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] [codex] +```
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] [codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] [codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] [codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] [codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] [codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] [codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 4 intentionally used no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials.
[codex] +The following checks for `currency-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and the `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure. The credential-free Phase 4 run
[codex] +   extracted all three internal declarations and 57 total occurrences, but
[codex] +   reported that a GitHub token was required for Actions lookups and could not
[codex] +   authenticate private Maven lookups; its 47 public candidate branches are
[codex] +   therefore only partial evidence.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that all three preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The credential-free
[codex] +   Phase 4 snapshot contained only 87 build/tool coordinates and zero
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer`, Spring Security, Tomcat, Netty, or PostgreSQL
[codex] +   coordinates because `serviceCommon` 0.0.16 was unavailable remotely. That
[codex] +   partial snapshot is a recorded limitation, not submission proof. Historical
[codex] +   build run `34027551741` used the same internal version successfully, but it
[codex] +   predates this workflow and is also not submission proof.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency validation.
[codex] +   Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose credentials to an
[codex] +   untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, and Netty findings where applicable. An
[codex] +   absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 4
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.

[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 4 intentionally used no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials.
[codex] +The following checks for `currency-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and the `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure. The credential-free Phase 4 run
[codex] +   extracted all three internal declarations and 57 total occurrences, but
[codex] +   reported that a GitHub token was required for Actions lookups and could not
[codex] +   authenticate private Maven lookups; its 47 public candidate branches are
[codex] +   therefore only partial evidence.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that all three preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The credential-free
[codex] +   Phase 4 snapshot contained only 87 build/tool coordinates and zero
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer`, Spring Security, Tomcat, Netty, or PostgreSQL
[codex] +   coordinates because `serviceCommon` 0.0.16 was unavailable remotely. That
[codex] +   partial snapshot is a recorded limitation, not submission proof. Historical
[codex] +   build run `34027551741` used the same internal version successfully, but it
[codex] +   predates this workflow and is also not submission proof.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency validation.
[codex] +   Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose credentials to an
[codex] +   untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, and Netty findings where applicable. An
[codex] +   absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 4
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.

[codex]       contents: write

[codex]         uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex]         with:

[codex]           java-version: '25'
[codex]           distribution: 'temurin'

[codex]           set -euo pipefail
[codex]

[codex]             exit 1
[codex]           fi
[codex]

[codex]           if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex]             echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex]             exit 1
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do

[codex]               --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex]               --output /dev/null \

[codex] # Dependency Automation
[codex] 
[codex] The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] failure triage are owned by
[codex] [orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Update discovery
[codex] 
[codex] `renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] `serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] `service-web`.
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] 
[codex] The Phase 4 local extraction found 57 dependency occurrences across eight files:
[codex] 37 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence, three Dockerfile
[codex] occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three workflows. Public
[codex] Gradle, wrapper, and image lookups produced candidate updates. GitHub Actions
[codex] lookups and the three `org.budgetanalyzer` Maven coordinates remain
[codex] authenticated hosted checks; the local runner had no GitHub or package-read
[codex] token, so those checks are pending rather than passed.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] `serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] fixed.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] 
[codex] `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] `basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] 
[codex] Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] `GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] `${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] 
[codex] The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] `service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Bot pull request checks
[codex] 
[codex] Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] approval.
[codex] 
[codex] Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] ./gradlew clean build
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] `pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] 
[codex] Phase 4 intentionally used no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials.
[codex] The following checks for `currency-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
